# U.S. news-media accounts on Sina Weibo

Generated: 2026-10-23T06:40:23.084293+00:00. Registry claims retain their own observation dates.

Candidate roster: **222 outlets; 222 reviewed; 0 pending**. This is an expandable research roster, not a census.
Officialness, historical existence, operator relationship, and current access are distinct.
Account totals are not independent outlet totals: one publisher can operate multiple channels. Press-release services and independent regional distributors are explicitly labeled; they are not presented as independent U.S. editorial newsrooms.
A visitor/login gate does not establish that an account was suspended or deleted.
**87 documented current/historical channels: 86 have resolved numeric UIDs; 1 do not.** A publisher-designated historical vanity URL establishes that channel, not its numeric identity or current survival. Current profile evidence does not establish present-day control or continuing licensing.

**Posts partially recovered for 68 of 87 documented channels.** 0 documented channels have no recorded collection attempt. Zero recovered posts after an attempt can reflect access, attribution or coverage limits, not zero account output.
There is no verified-negative outlet list: missing publisher links and access failures remain unknowns. The complete ranked and supplemental account-status ledger is linked in the [collection follow-up](collection_followup.md).
The [primary-source recheck](weibo_primary_source_audit.md) records source-level text corrections and distinguishes rechecked numeric identities from historical bindings still pending revalidation. A documented UID is not automatically a freshly revalidated identity.

### Evidence and operator notes

**ARTnews / licensed Chinese edition / ARTnews中文版** (confirmed). Operator: June2024 printed license credits name Life Style Media Group/Yimeizhiyuan (Beijing) Culture Media Co., Ltd.; current legal Weibo owner and later contracts unaudited. Owner blue-v certification explicitly identifies 《ARTnews中文版》杂志官方微博. Actual printed June2024 colophon independently states the Art Media, LLC license. Exact January23,2024 native4993344659259803 independently validates author UID and launch-caption publication; today's certification is not backdated. Four October22,2026 feed records must be excluded from the fixed interval.

- [artnews-actual-chinese-colophon-license](https://tangyuting.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/11/2024e5b9b46e69c88e5888ae794b5e5ad90e5888a.pdf) [publisher_statement; supports relationship; observed 2026-10-22T10:46:00.196227+00:00; claim date 2024-06-15]: Actual June2024 issue's printed colophon on PDF page4 explicitly states publication under license from Art Media, LLC, a PMC affiliate, naming Life Style Media Group/Yimeizhiyuan. Complete artist-hosted PDF is46,539,223bytes/67pages; primary evidence is the printed colophon, not the hosting artist's identity or an unverified search snippet. June15 is the issue's printed publication date, not the license's start/end or proof of continuous renewal.
- [artnews-current-institution](https://www.sina.cn/media/7832237238) [platform_certification; supports identity; observed 2026-10-22T10:42:27.983926+00:00; claim date unknown]: Owner title ARTnews中文版, blue-v institution badge and explicit 《ARTnews中文版》杂志官方微博 identify the publication's Chinese edition, not a generic art-news account, The Art Newspaper or a named editor.

This is an art/fashion publication and China operation of a U.S.-origin brand, not asserted a U.S. newsroom or an independent U.S.-ownership/current-license audit. PMC's own2019 announcement names Woruitai (Beijing) Culture Media as its licensed edition partner; planned August1 digital/September1 print dates are not asserted as verified completed launches. Current identity does not prove current activity, legal account control or license renewal; the2019 join date is not independently backdated certification. No candidate post is imported. Linked blog's static325-byte script points to blog_notopen controller; not executed/followed and not interpreted as Weibo deletion. No broadcaster funding/staffing/current-broadcast-status inference or join/last-post attribution cutoff. Review date: 2026-10-22 UTC. |

## Method and remaining gaps

Discovery prioritizes publisher-owned outbound links, explicit platform certification naming the outlet, and first-hand publisher confirmation. Search results and matching logos alone do not confirm officialness.

Collection uses the pilot-verified public Sina syndication feed, linked detail pages, and selected public Wayback profile snapshots, respecting robots rules and access failures. No Weibo gates are bypassed. Detail text is labeled source_full: the complete accessible syndicated body, not a guarantee of fidelity to an inaccessible original. Feed previews remain truncated in provenance. Reposts cannot reliably be identified on this source.

An attribution end excludes posts on or after that Asia/Shanghai date across live, archive and import routes, even when a UID retains the same name. These are evidence-backed collection cutoffs, not necessarily exact contract-termination dates; see each account's evidence and notes. An unestablished cutoff does not prove a continuing U.S. license.

No feed-history pagination has been demonstrated. Archive index hits alone are not recovered posts. Authorized/manual/archive JSONL imports can extend coverage, retaining publication versus capture dates. Resolve unknown UIDs before attempting account-specific syndication retrieval. No claim is made that any outlet lacking evidence never used Weibo.

Corpus text and raw responses are local-only by default. Public exports omit posts without explicit redistribution permission and list omissions. No third-party post text is reproduced in this report.
